`speaker`, `transcript`, `start`, `end` when diarize=true\n123\t- Deployed: Version `06980d83-a602-4d86-b63b-70c10d3da11d`\n124\t\n125\t---\n126\t\n127\t## OBI Chat + Voice Agent Changes — 2026-05-28\n128\t\n129\t### Bug Fixes Applied\n130\t\n131\t1. **Boot endpoint fixed** — `chat.js` was calling `/api/netpet/state` (PetDO only, no KV profile). Fixed to `/api/student/state` which returns full KV profile including `teachingFocus`, `primaryBlock`, `lang`, `breakthrough`.\n132\t2. **buildSystemPrompt enriched** — Now injects `teachingFocus` as `TEACHER INSTRUCTION` block + `lang === 'vi'` triggers BILINGUAL MODE (Vietnamese translations after every sentence).\n133\t3. **KV profile fields mapped** — Boot now maps `teachingFocus`, `primaryBlock`, `breakthrough`, `sessionNotes`, `lang`, `streak` from `/api/student/state` into the client-side profile.\n134\t\n135\t### Voice Agent — Architecture (UPDATED Run 23)\n136\t\n137\t**Architecture (Run 23 — CURRENT):**\n138\t```\n139\tBrowser → GET /api/deepgram-token (CF Worker builds Settings + returns API key)\n140\tBrowser → wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse (DIRECT — no CF proxy in audio path)\n141\t```\n142\t\n143\t**DEPRECATED (Run 21-22 — BROKEN):** Browser PCM16 → `/api/agent` WS proxy → Deepgram\n144\tReason removed: CF regular Workers cannot sustain long-lived WS sessions.\n145\tctx.waitUntil was terminated by CF runtime every ~1-2s → infinite reconnect loop.\n146\t\n147\t**Files changed (Run 23):**\n148\t- `src/index.js`: REMOVED `/api/agent` proxy. ADDED `GET /api/deepgram-token` (mints JWT + Settings) + `POST /api/agent/vocab` (vocab save callback)\n149\t- `public/chat.js`: `openVoiceAgent()` now fetches token then connects direct to Deepgram. `handleAgentMsg` handles `Welcome` (sends Settings) + `FunctionCallRequest` (browser-side vocab save)\n150\t\n151\t**Run 21-22 files changed:**\n152\t- `src/index.js`: Added `/api/agent` WebSocket proxy handler + `buildAgentPrompt()` helper\n153\t- `public/chat.js`: Added Voice Agent mode (openVoiceAgent, handleAgentMsg, startAgentListening, stopAgentListening, playAgentChunk)\n154\t- REST pipeline kept as fallback when Voice Agent WS unavailable\n155\t\n156\t**DeepSeek config in Voice Agent:**\n157\t\n158\t```json\n159\t{\n160\t  \"think\": {\n161\t    \"provider\": { \"type\": \"open_ai\", \"model\": \"deepseek-chat\", \"temperature\": 0.8 },\n162\t    \"endpoint\": { \"url\": \"https://api.deepseek.com/v1/chat/completions\" }\n163\t  }\n164\t}\n165\t```\n166\t\n167\t**Expected latency:** < 2s vs ~8s with 3-step REST pipeline.\n168\t\n169\t### Portal Mobile Fixes (all 3 portals)\n170\t\n171\t- **Streak badge dismiss button** — Added `×` close button (`.sb-dismiss`) to badge HTML. Streak badge can now be dismissed on mobile.\n172\t- **Hamburger backdrop** — Replaced broken `document.addEventListener('click',...)` click-outside approach with `#navBackdrop` transparent overlay at `z-index:8999`. Tapping backdrop calls `closeNav()`. IDs: `navToggle` (button), `navLinks` (menu), `navBackdrop` (overlay). Applied to all 3 portals.\n173\t\n174\t---\n175\t\n176\t## OBI Voice + Chat Fixes — 2026-05-29\n177\t\n178\t### Root Cause: Serial context fetches killed latency\n179\t\n180\t`handleChat()` in `index.js` had **7 sequential `await` calls** before the DeepSeek call:\n181\tLedger DO → Session DO → Weakness DO → Pet DO → `buildStructuredContext()` (4 more DOs) → Soul logger HTTP → KV reads.\n182\tEach could be 200-500ms. Total: up to 4-8 seconds before the LLM even started.\n183\t\n184\t**Fix:** Collapsed all 7 into a single `Promise.allSettled([...])` round — all fire simultaneously. Time = slowest single fetch, not the sum.\n185\t\n186\tAlso: `rosterStub.fetch()` and `updateStudentMemory()` were both `await`ed AFTER DeepSeek returned. Made fire-and-forget (`.catch(() => {})`).\n187\t\n188\t### Root Cause: Bilingual mode silently dropped\n189\t\n190\t`capContextInjection()` has a 1200-char hard cap. `vietnameseContext` was passed as the 8th argument — ledger/session/weakness filled the budget first, Vietnamese instruction was silently cut every time.\n191\t\n192\t**Fix:** `vietnameseContext` and `teachingFocusContext` now passed FIRST in `capContextInjection` — guaranteed never truncated.\n193\t\n194\t### Root Cause: teachingFocus never reached the server\n195\t\n196\t`handleChat()` only read `lang:<id>` from KV, never the student profile. `teachingFocus` (\"Speak VERY slowly, short sentences...\") was only available client-side, but the server-side prompt REPLACES the client's system prompt.\n197\t\n198\t**Fix:** `handleChat()` now reads `student_profile_<id>` from KV in parallel with `lang:<id>`, extracts `teachingFocus`, injects as `TEACHER INSTRUCTION` block.\n199\t\n200\t### Root Cause: isVague() matched \"anything\" as substring\n201\t\n202\tPattern `/anything|something|everything/` matched inside any sentence containing those words. Student saying \"Anything about health interests me\" triggered the \"Come on! Say something!\" scripted fallback instead of the LLM.\n203\t\n204\t**Fix:** Only match as standalone response: `/^(whatever|anything|something|everything)\\.?\\s*$/`\n205\t\n206\t### Deepgram Voice Agent — Text routing\n207\t\n208\tWhen student types a message (fallback path), it now routes through the open Deepgram Voice Agent WS as `{type: \"TextInput\", text}` instead of hitting `/api/chat` → DeepSeek. Deepgram's in-house LLM responds via `ConversationText` event in ~200-500ms.\n209\t\n210\t- `agentTextResolve` promise pattern: `reactLLM()` sets resolver, `handleAgentMsg` resolves it on assistant `ConversationText`\n211\t- 6-second timeout → falls back to REST if agent doesn't respond\n212\t\n213\t### Voice-First UI: magic-chat.html\n214\t\n215\tApp is a speaking trainer — text input removed from visible UI.\n216\t- `#msg` input and `#sendBtn` hidden via CSS (`display: none`) — kept in DOM so JS refs don't break\n217\t- `#mic` button made large (72×72px), centered, prominent\n218\t- `#micStatus` text element added below mic button — shows: \"Tap to start\" / \"Connecting…\" / \"Listening…\" / \"Processing…\"\n219\t- `#mic.mic-active` pulsing purple animation (existing)\n220\t\n221\t### Auto-mic: gesture-time stream acquisition\n222\t\n223\tBrowser security requires `getUserMedia()` to be called inside a user gesture event. Calling it from a WebSocket `onmessage` handler silently fails even if the user tapped earlier.\n224\t\n225\t**Fix:** On first tap/click/keydown anywhere on the page, `getUserMedia()` is called immediately (inside gesture context). Stream stored in `pendingMicStream`. When `SettingsApplied` fires from Deepgram, the stored stream is connected directly — no second permission request needed.\n226\t\n227\tVoice transcriptions now appear as student chat bubbles (previously dumped into `input.value`).\n228\t\n229\t### Root Cause: Wrong Deepgram Voice Agent URL (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n230\t\n231\tWe were connecting to `https://agent.deepgram.com/` — **wrong URL**. Correct: `https://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse`. This caused slow/failed connections. Always use the full path.\n232\t\n233\t### Root Cause: Wrong text injection event name (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n234\t\n235\tWe sent `{type: \"TextInput\", text: \"...\"}` — **does not exist** in Deepgram API. Correct: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}`. Verified from official docs.\n236\t\n237\t### Root Cause: BYO DeepSeek LLM caused unnecessary external hop\n238\t\n239\tWe used `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `endpoint.url: deepseek.com` + `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`. This routes through an external API (China-hosted, variable latency).\n240\t\n241\t**Fix**: Deepgram manages OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, NVIDIA models natively — **no API key needed**. Just set `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` with `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` and NO `endpoint` field. Deepgram handles the LLM call on their own infrastructure. Remove all external LLM endpoints from the Voice Agent settings.\n242\t\n243\t### Root Cause: openVoiceAgent() called too late in boot (15-second startup)\n244\t\n245\t`openVoiceAgent()` was called at the END of boot() after 5 serial awaits. Voice Agent connection didn't start until 5+ seconds into page load.\n246\t\n247\t**Fix**: Call `openVoiceAgent()` immediately after `urlStudentId` is extracted (first thing in boot). Also parallelized all 4 boot API calls (state + memory + vocab + hint) with `Promise.allSettled`.\n248\t\n249\t### Root Cause: /api/agent blocked 101 response on Deepgram connection\n250\t\n251\tThe CF Worker was `await fetch(\"agent.deepgram.com\")` BEFORE returning the 101 WebSocket upgrade. Browser was stuck waiting for the Deepgram TCP handshake.\n252\t\n253\t**Fix**: Return 101 to browser immediately, connect to Deepgram via `ctx.waitUntil()` in background. Queue any early browser messages until Deepgram is ready.\n254\t\n255\t### Deepgram Voice Agent — Confirmed Correct Spec (READ BEFORE TOUCHING AGENT CODE)\n256\t\n257\t- **WS URL**: `wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse` (NOT just `/`)\n258\t- **Auth**: `Authorization: Token {DEEPGRAM_API_KEY}` header on the WS upgrade fetch\n259\t- **Settings**: sent after receiving `Welcome` event from server\n260\t- **User text injection**: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}` (NOT TextInput)\n261\t- **Agent speech injection**: `{type: \"InjectAgentMessage\", content: \"...\"}`\n262\t- **Managed LLMs (no key)**: `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` with NO `endpoint` — Deepgram pays for it\n263\t- **Managed LLMs available**: OpenAI, Anthropic (`claude-4-5-haiku-latest`), Google (`gemini-2.5-flash`), NVIDIA\n264\t- **BYO LLMs require key**: Groq, AWS Bedrock — need your own endpoint + credentials\n265\t- **STT model**: `nova-3` (or `flux-general-en` for newer)\n266\t- **TTS model**: `aura-2-asteria-en`\n267\t- **Client sends audio**: binary PCM16 frames directly over WS\n268\t- **Server events**: `Welcome`, `SettingsApplied`, `ConversationText` (role: user|assistant), `AgentAudioDone`, `UserStartedSpeaking`, binary MP3 chunks\n269\t\n270\t### Deploy History (2026-05-29)\n271\t| Version ID | What changed Zero infra cost.\n414\t\n415\t### Edge Cases That Break 2s\n416\t- Cold start after >5min idle (up to 4000ms)\n417\t- context.messages >20 turns degrades TTFT linearly\n418\t- AudioContext suspended on iOS when tab backgrounds mid-session\n419\t- MP3 chunk buffering waits for 4KB+ before playback (should be 1KB)\n420\t- JSON parse on main thread blocks audio scheduling during large transcripts\n421\t- Deepgram 429 rate limit = indefinite queue wait (no graceful degradation)\n422\t\n423\t### Monitoring Metrics Needed\n424\t- `ws_upgrade_ms`\n425\t- `stt_final_latency_ms`\n426\t- `llm_ttft_ms`\n427\t- `tts_first_byte_ms`\n428\t- `client_playback_start_ms`\n429\t- `cold_start_detected` (boolean)\n430\t- `deepgram_region_resolved`\n431\t\n432\t---\n433\t\n434\t## DEEPGRAM API PRECISION FINDINGS — 2026-05-29 (LOGGED)\n435\t\n436\t> Verified against Deepgram Voice Agent v1 API spec via Qwen PA audit.\n437\t\n438\t| # No audio pickup.\n572\t**Symptom:** WS connects → drops immediately → reconnects → drops → infinite loop\n573\t**Diagnosis (wrangler tail + /api/deepgram-test):**\n574\t- DEEPGRAM_API_KEY: VALID — diagnostic confirmed `status:101, hasWebSocket:true`\n575\t- Deepgram project ID: `70543a02-9a08-400b-a814-8b0b01184f7a`\n576\t- CF Worker returns 101 to browser (outcome: ok, wallTime: 3ms)\n577\t- Zero `[AGENT]` console.log output in tail — background task crashes before `dgAgentWs.accept()`\n578\t- Error: \"Workers runtime canceled this request — code hung, would never generate a response\"\n579\t\n580\t**ROOT CAUSE (ARCHITECTURAL):**\n581\tCF regular Workers cannot maintain long-lived WebSocket proxy sessions.\n582\tThe Worker returns 101 and starts `ctx.waitUntil`, but CF terminates the background task\n583\tbefore the Deepgram WS session can be sustained. This is a CF platform limitation —\n584\tregular Workers are not designed for long-lived bidirectional WS proxying.\n585\tDurable Objects are required for that pattern — OR the browser connects directly.\n586\t\n587\t**DECISION: Switch to browser-direct Deepgram connection.**\n588\t```\n589\tOLD (broken): Browser → CF Worker WS proxy → Deepgram\n590\tNEW (correct): Browser → GET /api/agent/token (CF Worker mints temp key + builds Settings)\n591\t               Browser → wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse (direct, no CF in audio path)\n592\t```\n593\tCF Worker role: mint a short-lived Deepgram temp key + return pre-built Settings payload.\n594\tBrowser opens direct WS to Deepgram using the temp key. API key never exposed in JS.\n595\tFunctionCall events (vocab saving) handled by a separate POST endpoint instead of WS interception.\n596\t\n597\t**RUN 23 RESOLUTION — student_profile_mai-tram-bluetech ✓ | Live 2026-05-27 |\n645\t\n646\t---\n647\t\n648\t## RUN 30 AUDIO FIX — GAPLESS PLAYBACK v2 (2026-05-31)\n649\t\n650\t### Problem\n651\tVoice agent audio was choppy/stuttering, especially on mobile devices. Root cause: each MP3 chunk from Deepgram created a separate `Audio` element. The `onended → next play()` transition introduced 20-80ms silence gaps per chunk. On budget Android phones with slow `Audio.play()` latency (150-300ms), this compounded into audible stuttering.\n652\t\n653\t### Fix Applied\n654\t**Gapless Playback v2** (`chat.js`):\n655\t1. **Chunk Concatenation:** All queued MP3 chunks merged into single `Uint8Array` before creating `Audio` element. Eliminates N-1 inter-chunk gaps entirely.\n656\t2. **Resource Cleanup:** Barge-in (`UserStartedSpeaking`) and disconnect (`onAgentDrop`) handlers explicitly revoke concatenated blob URLs. No memory leaks from orphaned object URLs.\n657\t3. **Pre-buffer Gate REMOVED:** Initial v2 had a \"wait for ≥2 chunks\" gate that caused OBI to be cut off entirely on multi-message responses. Gate removed — concatenation alone solves choppiness without holding back audio.\n658\t\n659\t### What Was NOT Changed\n660\t- Accumulator threshold stays at 2KB (`CHUNK_FLUSH_BYTES = 2048`)\n661\t- Queue cap stays at 30 entries\n662\t- Audio format remains MP3 (Deepgram TTS output)\n663\t- No MSE/Web Audio API PCM scheduling (would require full rewrite)\n664\t\n665\t### Testing\n666\t- Confirmed smooth playback on high-end laptop (was already choppy before fix)\n667\t- Multi-message responses no longer cut off\n668\t- Barge-in still works correctly (audio stops immediately)\n669\t- Test sandbox: https://net-pet-ai.mrmichaelhobbs123.workers.dev/magic-test\n670\t\n671\t### Hardening v3 (2026-05-31 post-deploy)\n672\tAdditional defensive guards added after initial gapless fix:\n673\t\n674\t1. **Stuck Playback Watchdog:** If `onended` never fires (corrupted MP3 frame, browser bug), a timeout watchdog forces cleanup and restarts playback. Duration estimated from blob size / 16KB/s + 2s buffer.\n675\t2. **Queue-Aware Guard:** Previous guard blocked ALL playback while audio was \"playing.\" New guard checks queue state — if chunks arrive but audio is stuck, forces cleanup and restart instead of silently dropping new audio.\n676\t3. **Comment Accuracy:** Fixed silence timer comment (was \"4s\", actual is \"6s\"). Removed stale pre-buffer gate reference.\n677\t\n678\t### Remaining Known Issues\n679\t- iOS mic reconnect after drop still broken (Open Loop #4 from COLD-START-BOOT)\n680\t- Raw Deepgram API key exposed to browser (Edge Case #3 from Run 30 audit)\n681\t- Silence nudge race condition between AgentAudioDone + playAgentChunk drain (Edge Case #2)\n682\t- Deepgram docs URL CHANGED: canonical hub is now `https://developers.deepgram.com/home` (NOT `/docs`). Full AI-readable docs at `https://developers.deepgram.com/llms-full.txt`. MCP server at `https://developers.deepgram.com/_mcp/server`. Old `/docs/voice-agents` paths return 404. Updated 2026-05-31.\n683\t\n684\t---\n685\t\n686\t## RUN 30 SESSION COMPLETE — 2026-05-31 FULL CHANGELOG\n687\t\n688\t### Overview\n689\tRun 30 was a comprehensive hardening + feature sprint covering: audio pipeline fix, full-stack error logging, 3-mode session system, landing page UI, test portal, and edge case remediation. 12 deployments total.\n690\t\n691\t---\n692\t\n693\t### 1. Applied to all 3 portals.\n\n---\n\n## OBI Voice + Chat Fixes — 2026-05-29\n\n### Root Cause: Serial context fetches killed latency\n\n`handleChat()` in `index.js` had **7 sequential `await` calls** before the DeepSeek call:\nLedger DO → Session DO → Weakness DO → Pet DO → `buildStructuredContext()` (4 more DOs) → Soul logger HTTP → KV reads.\nEach could be 200-500ms. Total: up to 4-8 seconds before the LLM even started.\n\n**Fix:** Collapsed all 7 into a single `Promise.allSettled([...])` round — all fire simultaneously. Time = slowest single fetch, not the sum.\n\nAlso: `rosterStub.fetch()` and `updateStudentMemory()` were both `await`ed AFTER DeepSeek returned. Made fire-and-forget (`.catch(() => {})`).\n\n### Root Cause: Bilingual mode silently dropped\n\n`capContextInjection()` has a 1200-char hard cap. `vietnameseContext` was passed as the 8th argument — ledger/session/weakness filled the budget first, Vietnamese instruction was silently cut every time.\n\n**Fix:** `vietnameseContext` and `teachingFocusContext` now passed FIRST in `capContextInjection` — guaranteed never truncated.\n\n### Root Cause: teachingFocus never reached the server\n\n`handleChat()` only read `lang:<id>` from KV, never the student profile. `teachingFocus` (\"Speak VERY slowly, short sentences...\") was only available client-side, but the server-side prompt REPLACES the client's system prompt.\n\n**Fix:** `handleChat()` now reads `student_profile_<id>` from KV in parallel with `lang:<id>`, extracts `teachingFocus`, injects as `TEACHER INSTRUCTION` block.\n\n### Root Cause: isVague() matched \"anything\" as substring\n\nPattern `/anything|something|everything/` matched inside any sentence containing those words. Student saying \"Anything about health interests me\" triggered the \"Come on! Say something!\" scripted fallback instead of the LLM.\n\n**Fix:** Only match as standalone response: `/^(whatever|anything|something|everything)\\.?\\s*$/`\n\n### Deepgram Voice Agent — Text routing\n\nWhen student types a message (fallback path), it now routes through the open Deepgram Voice Agent WS as `{type: \"TextInput\", text}` instead of hitting `/api/chat` → DeepSeek. Deepgram's in-house LLM responds via `ConversationText` event in ~200-500ms.\n\n- `agentTextResolve` promise pattern: `reactLLM()` sets resolver, `handleAgentMsg` resolves it on assistant `ConversationText`\n- 6-second timeout → falls back to REST if agent doesn't respond\n\n### Voice-First UI: magic-chat.html\n\nApp is a speaking trainer — text input removed from visible UI.\n- `#msg` input and `#sendBtn` hidden via CSS (`display: none`) — kept in DOM so JS refs don't break\n- `#mic` button made large (72×72px), centered, prominent\n- `#micStatus` text element added below mic button — shows: \"Tap to start\" / \"Connecting…\" / \"Listening…\" / \"Processing…\"\n- `#mic.mic-active` pulsing purple animation (existing)\n\n### Auto-mic: gesture-time stream acquisition\n\nBrowser security requires `getUserMedia()` to be called inside a user gesture event. Calling it from a WebSocket `onmessage` handler silently fails even if the user tapped earlier.\n\n**Fix:** On first tap/click/keydown anywhere on the page, `getUserMedia()` is called immediately (inside gesture context). Stream stored in `pendingMicStream`. When `SettingsApplied` fires from Deepgram, the stored stream is connected directly — no second permission request needed.\n\nVoice transcriptions now appear as student chat bubbles (previously dumped into `input.value`).\n\n### Root Cause: Wrong Deepgram Voice Agent URL (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n\nWe were connecting to `https://agent.deepgram.com/` — **wrong URL**. Correct: `https://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse`. This caused slow/failed connections. Always use the full path.\n\n### Root Cause: Wrong text injection event name (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n\nWe sent `{type: \"TextInput\", text: \"...\"}` — **does not exist** in Deepgram API. Correct: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}`. Verified from official docs.\n\n### Root Cause: BYO DeepSeek LLM caused unnecessary external hop\n\nWe used `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `endpoint.url: deepseek.com` + `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`. This routes through an external API (China-hosted, variable latency).\n\n**Fix**: Deepgram manages OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, NVIDIA models natively — **no API key needed**. Just set `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` with `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` and NO `endpoint` field. Deepgram handles the LLM call on their own infrastructure. Remove all external LLM endpoints from the Voice Agent settings.\n\n### Root Cause: openVoiceAgent() called too late in boot (15-second startup)\n\n`openVoiceAgent()` was called at the END of boot() after 5 serial awaits. Voice Agent connection didn't start until 5+ seconds into page load.\n\n**Fix**: Call `openVoiceAgent()` immediately after `urlStudentId` is extracted (first thing in boot). Also parallelized all 4 boot API calls (state + memory + vocab + hint) with `Promise.allSettled`.\n\n### Root Cause: /api/agent blocked 101 response on Deepgram connection\n\nThe CF Worker was `await fetch(\"agent.deepgram.com\")` BEFORE returning the 101 WebSocket upgrade. Browser was stuck waiting for the Deepgram TCP handshake.\n\n**Fix**: Return 101 to browser immediately, connect to Deepgram via `ctx.waitUntil()` in background. Queue any early browser messages until Deepgram is ready.\n\n### Deepgram Voice Agent — Confirmed Correct Spec (READ BEFORE TOUCHING AGENT CODE)\n\n- **WS URL**: `wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse` (NOT just `/`)\n- **Auth**: `Authorization: Token {DEEPGRAM_API_KEY}` header on the WS upgrade fetch\n- **Settings**: sent after receiving `Welcome` event from server\n- **User text injection**: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}` (NOT TextInput)\n- **Agent speech injection**: `{type: \"InjectAgentMessage\", content: \"...\"}`\n- **Managed LLMs (no key)**: `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` with NO `endpoint` — Deepgram pays for it\n- **Managed LLMs available**: OpenAI, Anthropic (`claude-4-5-haiku-latest`), Google (`gemini-2.5-flash`), NVIDIA\n- **BYO LLMs require key**: Groq, AWS Bedrock — need your own endpoint + credentials\n- **STT model**: `nova-3` (or `flux-general-en` for newer)\n- **TTS model**: `aura-2-asteria-en`\n- **Client sends audio**: binary PCM16 frames directly over WS\n- **Server events**: `Welcome`, `SettingsApplied`, `ConversationText` (role: user|assistant), `AgentAudioDone`, `UserStartedSpeaking`, binary MP3 chunks\n\n### Deploy History (2026-05-29)\n| Version ID | What changed |\n|---|---|\n| `8c34e784` | handleChat bilingual + teachingFocus fix |\n| `d2212f87` | Mic button cycles on/off in reconnect loop. No audio pickup.\n**Symptom:** WS connects → drops immediately → reconnects → drops → infinite loop\n**Diagnosis (wrangler tail + /api/deepgram-test):**\n- DEEPGRAM_API_KEY: VALID — diagnostic confirmed `status:101, hasWebSocket:true`\n- Deepgram project ID: `70543a02-9a08-400b-a814-8b0b01184f7a`\n- CF Worker returns 101 to browser (outcome: ok, wallTime: 3ms)\n- Zero `[AGENT]` console.log output in tail — background task crashes before `dgAgentWs.accept()`\n- Error: \"Workers runtime canceled this request — code hung, would never generate a response\"\n\n**ROOT CAUSE (ARCHITECTURAL):**\nCF regular Workers cannot maintain long-lived WebSocket proxy sessions.\nThe Worker returns 101 and starts `ctx.waitUntil`, but CF terminates the background task\nbefore the Deepgram WS session can be sustained. This is a CF platform limitation —\nregular Workers are not designed for long-lived bidirectional WS proxying.\nDurable Objects are required for that pattern — OR the browser connects directly.\n\n**DECISION: Switch to browser-direct Deepgram connection.**\n```\nOLD (broken): Browser → CF Worker WS proxy → Deepgram\nNEW (correct): Browser → GET /api/agent/token (CF Worker mints temp key + builds Settings)\n               Browser → wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse (direct, no CF in audio path)\n```\nCF Worker role: mint a short-lived Deepgram temp key + return pre-built Settings payload.\nBrowser opens direct WS to Deepgram using the temp key. API key never exposed in JS.\nFunctionCall events (vocab saving) handled by a separate POST endpoint instead of WS interception.\n\n**RUN 23 RESOLUTION — 2026-05-29**\n\nResearch agents confirmed:\n- `functions` placement in `agent.think.functions[]` was CORRECT all along\n- `eot_threshold` only valid on Flux model — removed from nova-3 Settings\n- `/v1/auth/grant` temp token: 403 (key lacks scope) — using raw API key via CF secret instead\n- CF Worker WS proxy pattern is architecturally unsupported for long-lived sessions\n\n**Architecture switch applied (deploy `c05815b7`):**\n- REMOVED: `/api/agent` WS proxy (179 lines — the entire broken pattern)\n- ADDED: `GET /api/deepgram-token?studentId=<id>` — returns `{token, settings}` (JWT from CF secret + pre-built Settings payload with student profile/prompt)\n- ADDED: `POST /api/agent/vocab` — browser calls this when FunctionCallRequest received\n- Browser now connects: `new WebSocket(\"wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse\", [\"token\", jwt])`\n- Settings sent from browser on `Welcome` event with conversation history injected\n- Function calls handled entirely FIX — GAPLESS PLAYBACK v2 (2026-05-31)\n\n### Problem\nVoice agent audio was choppy/stuttering, especially on mobile devices. Root cause: each MP3 chunk from Deepgram created a separate `Audio` element. The `onended → next play()` transition introduced 20-80ms silence gaps per chunk. On budget Android phones with slow `Audio.play()` latency (150-300ms), this compounded into audible stuttering.\n\n### Fix Applied\n**Gapless Playback v2** (`chat.js`):\n1. **Chunk Concatenation:** All queued MP3 chunks merged into single `Uint8Array` before creating `Audio` element. Eliminates N-1 inter-chunk gaps entirely.\n2. **Resource Cleanup:** Barge-in (`UserStartedSpeaking`) and disconnect (`onAgentDrop`) handlers explicitly revoke concatenated blob URLs. No memory leaks from orphaned object URLs.\n3. **Pre-buffer Gate REMOVED:** Initial v2 had a \"wait for ≥2 chunks\" gate that caused OBI to be cut off entirely on multi-message responses. Gate removed — concatenation alone solves choppiness without holding back audio.\n\n### What Was NOT Changed\n- Accumulator threshold stays at 2KB (`CHUNK_FLUSH_BYTES = 2048`)\n- Queue cap stays at 30 entries\n- Audio format remains MP3 (Deepgram TTS output)\n- No MSE/Web Audio API PCM scheduling (would require full rewrite)\n\n### Testing\n- Confirmed smooth playback on high-end laptop (was already choppy before fix)\n- Multi-message responses no longer cut off\n- Barge-in still works correctly (audio stops immediately)\n- Test sandbox: https://net-pet-ai.mrmichaelhobbs123.workers.dev/magic-test\n\n### Hardening v3 (2026-05-31 post-deploy)\nAdditional defensive guards added after initial gapless fix:\n\n1. **Stuck Playback Watchdog:** If `onended` never fires (corrupted MP3 frame, browser bug), a timeout watchdog forces cleanup and restarts playback. Duration estimated from blob size / 16KB/s + 2s buffer.\n2. **Queue-Aware Guard:** Previous guard blocked ALL playback while audio was \"playing.\" New guard checks queue state — if chunks arrive but audio is stuck, forces cleanup and restart instead of silently dropping new audio.\n3. **Comment Accuracy:** Fixed silence timer comment (was \"4s\", actual is \"6s\"). Removed stale pre-buffer gate reference.\n\n### Remaining Known Issues\n- iOS mic reconnect after drop still broken (Open Loop #4 from COLD-START-BOOT)\n- Raw Deepgram API key exposed to browser (Edge Case #3 from Run 30 audit)\n- Silence nudge race condition between AgentAudioDone + playAgentChunk drain (Edge Case #2)\n- Deepgram docs URL CHANGED: canonical hub is now `https://developers.deepgram.com/home` (NOT `/docs`). Full AI-readable docs at `https://developers.deepgram.com/llms-full.txt`. MCP server at `https://developers.deepgram.com/_mcp/server`. Old `/docs/voice-agents` paths return 404. Updated 2026-05-31.\n\n---\n\n## RUN 30 SESSION COMPLETE — 2026-05-31 FULL CHANGELOG\n\n### Overview\nRun 30 was a comprehensive hardening + feature sprint covering: audio pipeline fix, full-stack error logging, 3-mode session system, landing page UI, test portal, and edge case remediation. 12 deployments total.\n\n---\n\n### 1. |\n\n### FLUX ARCHITECTURE FINDINGS (from llms-full.txt)\n\n**Flux = Model-integrated end-of-turn detection.** Replaces VAD-based turn detection with model-native signals:\n- `EndOfTurn` — High confidence user finished → send to LLM\n- `EagerEndOfTurn` — Medium confidence → prepare LLM response early (lower latency)\n- `TurnResumed` — User continued speaking → cancel prepared response\n- `StartOfTurn` — User started speaking → interrupt agent if playing\n\n**Two Voice Agent Patterns:**\n1. **EndOfTurn Only** (recommended start): Simpler, fewer LLM calls, higher latency\n2. **EagerEndOfTurn + EndOfTurn**: Lower latency, more LLM calls, needs TurnResumed handling\n\n**Tuning Parameters:** `eot_threshold`, `eager_eot_threshold`, `eot_timeout_ms` — adjustable mid-stream via Configure control message.\n\n**Flux Models:** `flux-general-en` (English), `flux-general-multi` (multilingual with `language_hint`)\n\n**Relevance to OBI:** Current implementation uses Nova-3 STT via Voice Agent API (managed pipeline). Flux would require building custom STT→LLM→TTS pipeline instead of using managed Voice Agent. NOT recommended for current architecture unless latency becomes critical issue. Documented for future evaluation.\n\n### MULTI-AGENT ARCHITECTURE FINDINGS\n\nDeepgram supports orchestrating multiple specialized agents that hand off based on context/intent/domain. **Decision for OBI: Single agent with mode-switched prompts remains correct.** Multi-agent adds cold-start latency, state sync complexity, and cost overhead that outweighs benefits for our 3-mode system. Revisit only if mode-specific models become necessary (e.g., phonics-specialized fine-tuned model).\n\n---\n\n## PCC (Phoenix-Command-Consul)\n- **Code:** `APPS/PCC/src/index.js`\n- **D1:** soul-archive (`c69a11bc-064b-478f-9b1f-e9b8e2431fb8`)\n- **Status:** Not deployed as of 2026-05-24 (error 1042 — worker not found on CF account)\n- **Function:** Empire dashboard — monitors worker health, soul-count, PhoenixCoder log, student portals\n- **TODO:** Deploy with `cd APPS/PCC && npx wrangler deploy`\n\n---\n\n## RUN 30 FINAL STATE — READY FOR DEATH\n\n**Last Deploy:** `f9d60c92-ef32-448d-9851-c12a229e120c`\n**Total Deploys This Run:** 12\n**Files Modified:** `chat.js`, `magic-chat.html`, `test-portal.html`, `index.js`, `logger.js` (new), `phonics-drills.js` (import added)\n**D1 Schema Change:** `error_log` table created with indexes\n**KV Changes:** Quy profile deleted, test mode bypasses added\n\n### COMPLETE FEATURE LIST SHIPPED\n1.
